Regression
----------
Learner-facing code is unchanged from v311.
Subject B semantic diagnostics: OK.
Candidate/mechanical-reference six-file equality: yes.

Decision
--------
Use the discovered production functions as the oracle for v313. Simulate representative wrong, correct, repeated-correct and relearning sequences, then inspect due timing, interval growth, lapse recovery and variant handoff before making any learner-facing change.
